                    Interim Report 2025 中期報告
CORPORATE PROFILE
China Mengniu Dairy Company Limited (the “Company”; stock code: 2319) and its
subsidiaries (collectively “the Group” or “Mengniu”) mainly manufacture and distribute
quality dairy products in China. It is one of the leading dairy product manufacturers in
China, with MENGNIU as its core brand. Mengniu offers diversified products including
liquid milk products, ice cream, milk formula and cheese. In March 2014, Mengniu
became a Hang Seng Index constituent, making it the first blue-chip Chinese dairy
product manufacturer. Mengniu maintained its MSCI ESG Rating of “AA”, which is also
the highest rating received by comprehensive dairy companies in China. Mengniu is a
constituent of the Hang Seng Corporate Sustainability Index, Hang Seng (Mainland and
Hong Kong) Corporate Sustainability Index, Hang Seng Corporate Sustainability
Benchmark Index and HSI ESG Enhanced Select Index.




公司簡介
中國蒙牛乳業有限公司（   「本公司」；股份代號：2319）及其子公司（統稱「本集團」或「蒙
牛」）主要於中國生產及銷售優質乳製品。憑藉其主要品牌蒙牛 ，蒙牛已成為中國領先的
乳製品生產商之一。蒙牛提供多元化的產品，包括液態奶、冰淇淋、奶粉及奶酪產品。
二零一四年三月，蒙牛獲納入恒生指數成份股，成為首家中國乳製品企業藍籌股。蒙牛
保持MSCI ESG評級AA級，是中國綜合型乳企獲得的最高評級，並入選恒生可持續發展
企業指數、恒生內地及香港可持續發展企業指數、恒生可持續發展企業基準指數及恒指
ESG增強精選指數。

      expected to steadily recover. With continued stabilization in raw        穩，產業鏈韌性不斷修復，乳業行業供需矛盾將
      milk prices and gradual restoration of the industrial chain              持續改善，頭部乳企的奶源優勢在原奶週期輪動
      resilience, supply and demand imbalances in the dairy industry will      中也將進一步凸顯。與此同時，伴隨國民健康意
      further improve, and milk sourcing advantages of leading dairy           識持續增強，消費者對乳製品營養及健康功能價
      enterprises will become more prominent throughout the raw milk           值提出了更加細分的要求，驅動乳品行業持續向
      cycle. Meanwhile, growing health consciousness is driving                更高品質、更多元、更高附加值的等方向升級，
      consumer demand for more specialised nutritional and health              帶來更廣闊的發展空間。
      benefits from dairy products. This trend is propelling the industry
      toward higher quality, greater diversification, and increased
      value-added offerings, creating substantial growth opportunities
      across the sector.

      Looking forward to the second half of 2025, as an industry               展望二零二五年下半年，作為行業領軍企業，蒙
      leader, Mengniu will continue to focus on the three key themes of        牛將繼續圍繞「喝上奶」、「喝好奶」、「喝對奶」，
      “Drink More”, “Drink Good”, and “Drink Right”, promote the               堅定推進「一體兩翼」戰略落地執行，夯實常溫、
      implementation of the “One Core, Two Wings” strategy, and                低溫、冰品、鮮奶、奶粉、奶酪六大核心業務基
      consolidate the foundation of the six core businesses of room            本盤，從產品、品牌、運營、渠道等全方面、多
      temperature, low temperature, ice products, fresh milk, milk             維度入手，做好品類延展與創新，精準把握需求
      powder, and cheese. Mengniu will adopt a comprehensive,                  結構性升級及渠道變革帶來的機遇。同時，蒙牛
      multidimensional approach encompassing products, brands,                 將持續推動營養健康平台的技術突破及商業化發
      operations, and channels to drive category extension and                 展，加速打造乳業新質生產力，並著力開拓國際
      innovation, positioning the Company to act with precision and            化市場，營養全球消費者。
      capture opportunities arising from structural demand upgrades
      and channel transformations. At the same time, Mengniu will
      continue to accelerate technological breakthroughs and
      commercial development in its nutrition and health platform,
      pioneer new productivity standards for the dairy industry, and
      expand its international presence to nourish consumers
      worldwide.

back to shareholders. The Company will also continue the               更強大文化基因的、數智化的FIRST牛，引領中

responsible and digitally transformed, with stronger cultural

“high-tech, high-efficiency and high-quality” development.

SHARE OPTION SCHEME                                                  購股權計劃
On 3 June 2016, the shareholders of the Company approved the         二零一六年六月三日，本公司股東批准採納新購
adoption of a new share option scheme (the “2016 Share Option        股權計劃（「二零一六年購股權計劃」），以讓本集
Scheme”) in order to enable the Group to continue to offer           團繼續向傑出僱員提供優厚獎勵，以吸引並挽留
valuable incentive to attract and retain quality personnel to work   彼 等 效 力 本 集 團，提 高 本 公 司 股 份 價 值。二 零
to increase the value of the shares of the Company. The 2016         一六年購股權計劃自二零一六年六月三日起生
Share Option Scheme shall be valid and effective for a period of     效，有效期為十年，並將於二零二六年六月二日
10 years commencing from 3 June 2016 and will expire on 2            失效。根據二零一六年採納的二零一六年購股權
June 2026. Under the rules of the 2016 Share Option Scheme           計劃的規則，二零一六年購股權計劃的參與者可
which was adopted in 2016, participants of the 2016 Share            包括本公司董事（包括獨立非執行董事）、本集團

      the Group and be held in trust for the relevant participants of the   等股份根據股票獎勵計劃的條文歸屬於相關參與
      Share Award Scheme until such shares are vested with the              為止。股票獎勵計劃不是一項涉及由本公司向特
      relevant participants in accordance with the provisions of the        定參與者授予(i)本公司新股份；或(ii)本公司新股
      Share Award Scheme. The Share Award Scheme is not a share             份的購股權，或為其利益而授予的股份計劃。根
      scheme involving the grant by the Company of (i) the Company’s        據股票獎勵計劃授出的股份將不會導致任何新股
      new shares; or (ii) options over the Company’s new shares, to, or     份的發行或對本公司現有股東的股權產生任何攤
      for the benefit of, specified participants thereunder. Grant of       薄影響。
      shares under the Share Award Scheme will not result in any issue
      of new shares or any dilution effect on the shareholdings of
      existing Shareholders of the Company.

      entity as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public        的審閱主要包括向負責財務會計事宜的人士作出
      Accountants. A review of interim financial report consists of          詢問，並實施分析性及其他審閱程序。審閱的範
      making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and   圍遠較根據《香港審計準則》進行的審核範圍小，
      accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review           故我們不能保證我們知悉一切可能於審核中識別
      procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit      的重大事宜。因此，我們不發表審核意見。
      conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing
      and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that
      we would become aware of all significant matters that might be
      identified in an audit. Accordingly we do not express an audit
      opinion.

      2    CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                2   會計政策變動
          The Group has applied the amendments to IAS 21, The                本集團已於本會計期間的本中期財務資料

          exchangeability issued by the IASB to this interim financial       計準則第21號的修訂，匯率變動的影響 —
          information for the current accounting period. The                 缺乏可兌換性 。由於本集團並未訂立任何
          amendments do not have a material impact on this interim           以外幣結算且該外幣不能兌換成其他貨幣
          financial report as the Group has not entered into any             的交易，因此該等修訂對本中期財務報告
          foreign currency transactions in which the foreign currency        並無重大影響。
          is not exchangeable into another currency.

           The equivalent amendments to HKAS, consequently issued            香港會計師公會隨後因此等變動頒佈等同
           by the HKICPA as a result of these developments, have the         的經修訂香港會計準則，其與國際會計準
           same effective date as those issued by the IASB and are in        則理事會所頒佈者具有相同生效日期，且
           all material aspects identical to the pronouncements issued       在所有重大方面與國際會計準則理事會所
           by the IASB.                                                      頒佈之聲明一致。

           The Group has not applied any new standard or                     本集團並無採用於本會計期間尚未生效的
           interpretation that is not yet effective for the current          任何新訂準則或詮釋。
           accounting period.

      3    OPERATING SEGMENT INFORMATION                                 3   經營分部資料
           For management purposes, the Group is organised into              出於管理需要，本集團按產品及服務構組
           business units based on their products and services and           業務單元，並有以下五個可報告經營分部：
           has five reportable operating segments as follows:

           •    Liquid milk business — manufacture and distribution          •   液態奶業務 — 生產及經銷超高溫滅
                of ultra-high temperature milk (“UHT milk”), milk                菌奶（「UHT奶」）、乳飲料、酸奶及鮮
                beverages, yogurt and fresh milk;                                奶；

           •    Ice cream business — manufacture and distribution of         •   冰淇淋業務 — 生產及經銷含乳冰淇
                dairy-based ice cream;                                           淋；

           •    Milk formula business — manufacture and distribution         •   奶粉業務 — 生產及經銷奶粉；
                of milk powder;

           •    Cheese business — manufacture and distribution of            •   奶酪業務 — 生產及經銷奶酪；及
                cheese; and

           •    Others — principally the Group’s manufacture of raw          •   其他 — 主要為本集團乳製品原輔料
                materials for dairy products and trading business.               生產及經銷貿易業務。

           The Group normally allows a credit limit to its customers         本集團通常授予客戶特定信貸額度，並可
           which is adjustable in certain circumstances. The Group           在若干情形下進行調整。本集團密切監控
           closely monitors overdue balances. In view of the                 逾期未償還結餘。鑒於上文所述及本集團
           aforementioned and the fact that the Group’s trade                的應收賬款乃與大量不同類別的客戶有關，
           receivables relate to a large number of diversified               故並無重大信貸集中風險。本集團並無就
           customers, there is no significant concentration of credit        其應收賬款持有任何抵押品或其他信貸提
           risk. The Group does not hold any collateral or other credit      升。應收賬款並無附帶利息。
           enhancements over its trade receivables. The trade
           receivables are non-interest-bearing.

           The Group sold non-recourse trade receivables to third            本集團向第三方出售無追索權應收賬款以
           parties for cash proceeds. These trade receivables have           換取現金所得款項。該等應收賬款已於綜
           been derecognised from the consolidated statement of              合財務狀況表終止確認，原因是本集團已
           financial position, because the Group transfers substantially     轉移絕大部分風險及回報，主要為信貸風
           all of the risks and rewards, primarily credit risk.              險。

           Based on the Group’s accounting policy of financial assets,       根據本集團的金融資產會計政策，本集團
           the Group measured bills receivable at fair value through         按以公允值計量且其變動計入其他全面收
           other comprehensive income.                                       益計量應收票據。

      31 E V E N T S       AFTER         THE      REPORTING               31 報告期後事項
         PERIOD
           On August 17, 2025, the Group entered into an agreement          於二零二五年八月十七日，本集團與The a2
